Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any protocols module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 129. The projective resolution has length at most 658 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the protocols space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 129 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 658. QED.
